1	import type { SendRequest } from '@/features/send';
2	import type {
3	  TaskDefinition,
4	  TaskInstanceState,
5	  TaskProgress,
6	  TaskStepResult,
7	  WaitCondition,
8	  ConditionMatchInput,
9	} from '../core';
10	import {
11	  transition,
12	  isTerminal,
13	  evaluateCondition,
14	} from '../core';
15	import type { SendServiceProvider, ReceiveEventSource } from '../adapters';
16	import type { TaskStateContainer, TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';
17	import { createTaskState } from '../state';
18	import {
19	  selectTaskInstance,
20	  selectTaskProgress,
21	  selectTaskSnapshot,
22	  selectTaskStatistics,
23	} from '../selectors';
24	
25	// --- Public interfaces ---
26	
27	export interface TaskReader {
28	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
29	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
30	  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined;
31	  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
32	}
33	
34	export interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
35	  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
36	  startTask(instanceId: string): void;
37	  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;
38	  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;
39	  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;
40	  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;
41	  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;
42	}
43	
44	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
45	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
46	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
47	  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;
48	  readonly now?: () => string;
49	}
50	
51	// --- Condition registry ---
52	
53	interface ConditionEntry {
54	  readonly condition: WaitCondition;
55	  readonly onMatch: (value: number | string) => void;
56	}
57	
58	class ConditionRegistry {
59	  private entries = new Map<string, Set<ConditionEntry>>();
60	
61	  register(condition: WaitCondition, onMatch: (value: number | string) => void): ConditionEntry {
62	    const entry: ConditionEntry = { condition, onMatch };
63	    const key = condition.frameId;
64	    if (!this.entries.has(key)) {
65	      this.entries.set(key, new Set());
66	    }
67	    this.entries.get(key)!.add(entry);
68	    return entry;
69	  }
70	
71	  unregister(entry: ConditionEntry): void {
72	    const key = entry.condition.frameId;
73	    const set = this.entries.get(key);
74	    if (set) {
75	      set.delete(entry);
76	      if (set.size === 0) {
77	        this.entries.delete(key);
78	      }
79	    }
80	  }
81	
82	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
83	    const set = this.entries.get(input.frameId);
84	    if (!set) return;
85	    for (const entry of set) {
86	      if (evaluateCondition(entry.condition, input)) {
87	        entry.onMatch(input.value as number | string);
88	      }
89	    }
90	  }
91	
92	  clear(): void {
93	    this.entries.clear();
94	  }
95	
96	  get size(): number {
97	    let total = 0;
98	    for (const set of this.entries.values()) {
99	      total += set.size;
100	    }
101	    return total;
102	  }
103	}
104	
105	// --- Helpers ---
106	
107	function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
108	  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
109	}
110	
111	function buildSendRequest(
112	  stepConfig: TaskDefinition['steps'][number] & { kind: 'send' },
113	  definition: TaskDefinition,
114	  stepIndex: number,
115	): SendRequest {
116	  return {
117	    frameId: stepConfig.sendConfig.frameId,
118	    fieldValues: stepConfig.sendConfig.fieldValues,
119	    targetId: stepConfig.sendConfig.targetId ?? definition.targetId ?? '',
120	    options: stepConfig.sendConfig.options ?? {},
121	    context: { source: 'task', taskId: definition.id, stepIndex },
122	  };
123	}
124	
125	// --- Factory ---
126	
127	let nextInstanceId = 1;
128	
129	export function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService {
130	  const sendService = options.sendService;
131	  const receiveSource = options.receiveEventSource;
132	  const state = options.state ?? createTaskState();
133	  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
134	
135	  // Execution control
136	  const abortResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
137	  const settleResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
138	
139	  // Condition matching
140	  const conditionRegistry = new ConditionRegistry();
141	  let receiveSubscription: (() => void) | null = null;
142	  let subscriptionRefCount = 0;
143	
144	  // --- Subscription management ---
145	
146	  function ensureSubscription(): void {
147	    if (receiveSubscription) {
148	      subscriptionRefCount++;
149	      return;
150	    }
151	    receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {
152	      conditionRegistry.processInput(input);
153	    });
154	    subscriptionRefCount = 1;
155	  }
156	
157	  function releaseSubscription(): void {
158	    subscriptionRefCount--;
159	    if (subscriptionRefCount <= 0 && receiveSubscription) {
160	      receiveSubscription();
161	      receiveSubscription = null;
162	      subscriptionRefCount = 0;
163	    }
164	  }
165	
166	  // --- Abort / settle ---
167	
168	  function createAbortSignal(instanceId: string): Promise<void> {
169	    return new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
170	      abortResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
171	    });
172	  }
173	
174	  function abortInstance(instanceId: string): void {
175	    const resolve = abortResolvers.get(instanceId);
176	    if (resolve) {
177	      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
178	      resolve();
179	    }
180	  }
181	
182	  function resolveSettle(instanceId: string): void {
183	    const resolve = settleResolvers.get(instanceId);
184	    if (resolve) {
185	      settleResolvers.delete(instanceId);
186	      resolve();
187	    }
188	  }
189	
190	  // --- Lifecycle helpers ---
191	
192	  function updateLifecycle(
193	    instanceId: string,
194	    action: 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail',
195	    extra?: Partial<TaskInstanceState>,
196	  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined {
197	    const instance = state.getInstance(instanceId);
198	    if (!instance) return undefined;
199	    const newStatus = transition(instance.lifecycle, action);
200	    const timestampField =
201	      action === 'start' ? 'startedAt' :
202	      action === 'pause' ? 'pausedAt' :
203	      action === 'stop' ? 'stoppedAt' :
204	      action === 'complete' ? 'completedAt' :
205	      action === 'fail' ? 'failedAt' : undefined;
206	    return state.updateInstance(instanceId, {
207	      lifecycle: newStatus,
208	      ...(timestampField ? { [timestampField]: now() } : {}),
209	      ...extra,
210	    });
211	  }
212	
213	  function completeTask(instanceId: string): void {
214	    updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'complete');
215	    conditionRegistry.clear();
216	    resolveSettle(instanceId);
217	  }
218	
219	  function failTask(instanceId: string, error: string): void {
220	    updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error });
221	    conditionRegistry.clear();
222	    resolveSettle(instanceId);
223	  }
224	
225	  // --- Step execution ---
226	
227	  async function executeSendStep(
228	    instanceId: string,
229	    step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'send' }>,
230	    iteration: number,
231	    stepIndex: number,
232	    definition: TaskDefinition,
233	  ): Promise<TaskStepResult> {
234	    const request = buildSendRequest(step, definition, stepIndex);
235	    const sendResult = await sendService.execute(request);
236	    return { kind: 'send', stepIndex, iteration, sendResult };
237	  }
238	
239	  async function executeWaitConditionStep(
240	    instanceId: string,
241	    step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'wait-condition' }>,
242	    iteration: number,
243	    stepIndex: number,
244	    signal: Promise<void>,
245	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; interrupted: boolean }> {
246	    const { condition, timeoutMs } = step.waitConfig;
247	
248	    return new Promise((resolve) => {
249	      let settled = false;
250	
251	      const entry = conditionRegistry.register(condition, (value) => {
252	        if (settled) return;
253	        settled = true;
254	        conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);
255	        resolve({
256	          result: {
257	            kind: 'wait-condition',
258	            stepIndex,
259	            iteration,
260	            matched: true,
261	            matchedValue: value,
262	            timedOut: false,
263	          },
264	          interrupted: false,
265	        });
266	      });
267	
268	      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
269	        if (settled) return;
270	        settled = true;
271	        conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);
272	        resolve({
273	          result: {
274	            kind: 'wait-condition',
275	            stepIndex,
276	            iteration,
277	            matched: false,
278	            timedOut: true,
279	          },
280	          interrupted: false,
281	        });
282	      }, timeoutMs);
283	
284	      signal.then(() => {
285	        if (settled) return;
286	        settled = true;
287	        clearTimeout(timer);
288	        conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);
289	        resolve({
290	          result: {
291	            kind: 'wait-condition',
292	            stepIndex,
293	            iteration,
294	            matched: false,
295	            timedOut: false,
296	          },
297	          interrupted: true,
298	        });
299	      });
300	    });
301	  }
302	
303	  async function executeDelayStep(
304	    iteration: number,
305	    stepIndex: number,
306	    durationMs: number,
307	    signal: Promise<void>,
308	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; interrupted: boolean }> {
309	    const race = await Promise.race([
310	      sleep(durationMs).then(() => 'done' as const),
311	      signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
312	    ]);
313	    return {
314	      result: { kind: 'delay', stepIndex, iteration, completed: race === 'done' },
315	      interrupted: race === 'interrupted',
316	    };
317	  }
318	
319	  async function applyErrorPolicy(
320	    instanceId: string,
321	    failedResult: TaskStepResult,
322	    policy: TaskDefinition['errorPolicy'],
323	    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
324	    iteration: number,
325	    stepIndex: number,
326	    definition: TaskDefinition,
327	    signal: Promise<void>,
328	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
329	    switch (policy.onFailure) {
330	      case 'stop':
331	        updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
332	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'stop' }, shouldStop: true };
333	
334	      case 'pause':
335	        updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
336	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'pause' }, shouldStop: true };
337	
338	      case 'skip-step':
339	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
340	
341	      case 'retry': {
342	        const maxRetries = policy.retryCount ?? 0;
343	        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxRetries; attempt++) {
344	          if (policy.retryDelayMs && policy.retryDelayMs > 0) {
345	            const race = await Promise.race([
346	              sleep(policy.retryDelayMs).then(() => false),
347	              signal.then(() => true),
348	            ]);
349	            if (race) return null;
350	          }
351	
352	          const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
353	          if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return null;
354	
355	          // Re-execute raw step without re-entering error policy
356	          let retryResult: TaskStepResult;
357	          switch (step.kind) {
358	            case 'send':
359	              retryResult = await executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
360	              break;
361	            case 'wait-condition': {
362	              const wc = await executeWaitConditionStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal);
363	              if (wc.interrupted) return null;
364	              retryResult = wc.result;
365	              break;
366	            }
367	            case 'delay': {
368	              const d = await executeDelayStep(iteration, stepIndex, step.delayConfig.durationMs, signal);
369	              if (d.interrupted) return null;
370	              retryResult = d.result;
371	              break;
372	            }
373	          }
374	
375	          if (!isStepFailed(retryResult)) {
376	            return { result: retryResult, shouldStop: false };
377	          }
378	        }
379	        // Exhausted retries
380	        updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail', { error: 'Retry exhausted' });
381	        return { result: { ...failedResult, appliedPolicy: 'retry' }, shouldStop: true };
382	      }
383	    }
384	  }
385	
386	  async function executeStepCore(
387	    instanceId: string,
388	    step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
389	    iteration: number,
390	    stepIndex: number,
391	    definition: TaskDefinition,
392	    signal: Promise<void>,
393	  ): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
394	    switch (step.kind) {
395	      case 'send': {
396	        const result = await executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
397	        if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
398	          return applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
399	        }
400	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
401	      }
402	
403	      case 'wait-condition': {
404	        const { result, interrupted } = await executeWaitConditionStep(
405	          instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,
406	        );
407	        if (interrupted) return null;
408	
409	        if (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {
410	          switch (step.waitConfig.onTimeout) {
411	            case 'continue':
412	              return { result, shouldStop: false };
413	            case 'skip':
414	              return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
415	            case 'fail':
416	              return applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition, signal);
417	          }
418	        }
419	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
420	      }
421	
422	      case 'delay': {
423	        const { result, interrupted } = await executeDelayStep(
424	          iteration, stepIndex, step.delayConfig.durationMs, signal,
425	        );
426	        if (interrupted) return null;
427	        return { result, shouldStop: false };
428	      }
429	    }
430	  }
431	
432	  function isStepFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {
433	    switch (result.kind) {
434	      case 'send': return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
435	      case 'wait-condition': return !result.matched && result.timedOut;
436	      case 'delay': return !result.completed;
437	    }
438	  }
439	
440	  // --- executeSteps ---
441	
442	  async function executeSteps(
443	    instanceId: string,
444	    iteration: number,
445	    steps: readonly TaskDefinition['steps'],
446	    definition: TaskDefinition,
447	    signal: Promise<void>,
448	  ): Promise<boolean> {
449	    // Determine start index: skip already-completed steps in this iteration
450	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
451	    if (!inst) return false;
452	    const completedInIter = inst.stepResults
453	      .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)
454	      .map((r) => r.stepIndex);
455	    const startIndex = completedInIter.length;
456	
457	    for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {
458	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
459	      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;
460	
461	      state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });
462	
463	      const outcome = await executeStepCore(
464	        instanceId, steps[i], iteration, i, definition, signal,
465	      );
466	
467	      if (outcome === null) return false;
468	
469	      state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);
470	
471	      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;
472	    }
473	    return true;
474	  }
475	
476	  // --- Scheduling loops ---
477	
478	  async function runTimedLoop(
479	    instanceId: string,
480	    definition: TaskDefinition,
481	    signal: Promise<void>,
482	  ): Promise<void> {
483	    const { intervalMs, maxCount, delayBeforeStartMs } = definition;
484	    const maxIter = maxCount ?? Infinity;
485	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
486	    const startIteration = inst?.currentIteration ?? 0;
487	
488	    if (delayBeforeStartMs && delayBeforeStartMs > 0 && startIteration === 0) {
489	      const race = await Promise.race([
490	        sleep(delayBeforeStartMs).then(() => 'done' as const),
491	        signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
492	      ]);
493	      if (race === 'interrupted') return;
494	    }
495	
496	    for (let iter = startIteration; iter < maxIter; iter++) {
497	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
498	      if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
499	
500	      const completed = await executeSteps(
501	        instanceId, iter, definition.steps, definition, signal,
502	      );
503	
504	      if (!completed) return;
505	
506	      const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);
507	      if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
508	
509	      // Not last iteration → wait for interval
510	      if (iter + 1 < maxIter) {
511	        const race = await Promise.race([
512	          sleep(intervalMs ?? 1000).then(() => 'done' as const),
513	          signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
514	        ]);
515	        if (race === 'interrupted') return;
516	      }
517	    }
518	  }
519	
520	  async function runTriggerLoop(
521	    instanceId: string,
522	    definition: TaskDefinition,
523	    signal: Promise<void>,
524	  ): Promise<void> {
525	    const triggerCondition = definition.triggerCondition;
526	    if (!triggerCondition) return;
527	
528	    const maxTriggerCount = definition.maxTriggerCount ?? Infinity;
529	    const cooldownMs = definition.cooldownMs ?? 0;
530	    let triggerCount = 0;
531	    let lastTriggerTime = 0;
532	    const triggerQueue: (number | string)[] = [];
533	    let queueNotify: (() => void) | null = null;
534	
535	    const handler = (_value: number | string) => {
536	      const currentTime = Date.now();
537	      if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && currentTime - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
538	      if (triggerCount + triggerQueue.length >= maxTriggerCount) return;
539	
540	      lastTriggerTime = currentTime;
541	      triggerQueue.push(_value);
542	      if (queueNotify) {
543	        queueNotify();
544	        queueNotify = null;
545	      }
546	    };
547	
548	    const entry = conditionRegistry.register(triggerCondition, handler);
549	
550	    try {
551	      while (triggerCount < maxTriggerCount) {
552	        const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
553	        if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') break;
554	
555	        // Wait for queued trigger
556	        if (triggerQueue.length === 0) {
557	          const notified = await Promise.race([
558	            new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {
559	              queueNotify = () => resolve(true);
560	            }),
561	            signal.then(() => false),
562	          ]);
563	          if (!notified) break;
564	        }
565	
566	        triggerQueue.shift();
567	        triggerCount++;
568	
569	        await executeSteps(
570	          instanceId, triggerCount - 1, definition.steps, definition, signal,
571	        );
572	
573	        const afterExec = state.getInstance(instanceId);
574	        if (!afterExec || afterExec.lifecycle !== 'running') break;
575	      }
576	    } finally {
577	      conditionRegistry.unregister(entry);
578	    }
579	  }
580	
581	  async function runSequenceLoop(
582	    instanceId: string,
583	    definition: TaskDefinition,
584	    signal: Promise<void>,
585	  ): Promise<void> {
586	    await executeSteps(instanceId, 0, definition.steps, definition, signal);
587	  }
588	
589	  async function runExecutionLoop(
590	    instanceId: string,
591	    signal: Promise<void>,
592	  ): Promise<void> {
593	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
594	    if (!inst) return;
595	
596	    try {
597	      ensureSubscription();
598	      switch (inst.definitionRef.schedulingMode) {
599	        case 'timed':
600	          await runTimedLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
601	          break;
602	        case 'trigger':
603	          await runTriggerLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
604	          break;
605	        case 'sequence':
606	          await runSequenceLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
607	          break;
608	      }
609	
610	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
611	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
612	        completeTask(instanceId);
613	      }
614	    } catch (err) {
615	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
616	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
617	        failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
618	      }
619	    } finally {
620	      releaseSubscription();
621	      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
622	    }
623	  }
624	
625	  // --- Public API ---
626	
627	  return {
628	    getSnapshot() {
629	      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());
630	    },
631	
632	    getInstance(instanceId) {
633	      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
634	    },
635	
636	    getProgress(instanceId) {
637	      return selectTaskProgress(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
638	    },
639	
640	    getStatistics() {
641	      return selectTaskStatistics(state.getSnapshot());
642	    },
643	
644	    createTask(definition) {
645	      const instanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
646	      return state.createInstance(instanceId, definition);
647	    },
648	
649	    startTask(instanceId) {
650	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
651	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return;
652	
653	      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'start');
654	
655	      const signal = createAbortSignal(instanceId);
656	
657	      // Fire-and-forget execution loop
658	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
659	    },
660	
661	    pauseTask(instanceId) {
662	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
663	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
664	
665	      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
666	      conditionRegistry.clear();
667	      abortInstance(instanceId);
668	    },
669	
670	    resumeTask(instanceId) {
671	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
672	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
673	
674	      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'resume');
675	
676	      const signal = createAbortSignal(instanceId);
677	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
678	    },
679	
680	    stopTask(instanceId) {
681	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
682	      if (!inst) return;
683	      if (inst.lifecycle !== 'running' && inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
684	
685	      updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
686	      conditionRegistry.clear();
687	      abortInstance(instanceId);
688	    },
689	
690	    removeTask(instanceId) {
691	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
692	      if (!inst) return;
693	      if (!isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
694	
695	      abortInstance(instanceId);
696	      state.removeInstance(instanceId);
697	    },
698	
699	    async onSettled(instanceId) {
700	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
701	      if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
702	
703	      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
704	        settleResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
705	      });
706	    },
707	  };
708	}
709	
```

1	import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';
2	import type { ReceiveEventSource } from './ports';
3	
4	export interface FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions {
5	  readonly autoEmit?: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
6	  readonly autoEmitIntervalMs?: number;
7	}
8	
9	export function createFakeReceiveEventSource(): ReceiveEventSource & {
10	  emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void;
11	  readonly emitted: readonly ConditionMatchInput[];
12	} {
13	  const emitted: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
14	  const handlers = new Set<(input: ConditionMatchInput) => void>();
15	
16	  return {
17	    get emitted() {
18	      return emitted;
19	    },
20	
21	    emit(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
22	      emitted.push(input);
23	      for (const handler of handlers) {
24	        handler(input);
25	      }
26	    },
27	
28	    subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void {
29	      handlers.add(handler);
30	      return () => {
31	        handlers.delete(handler);
32	      };
33	    },
34	  };
35	}
36

1	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
2	import type {
3	  TaskDefinition,
4	  TaskInstanceState,
5	  TaskStepResult,
6	} from '../core';
7	import { cloneInstanceState, cloneStepResult } from '../core';
8	
9	const MAX_HISTORY = 50;
10	const DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS = 100;
11	
12	// --- Statistics ---
13	
14	export interface TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
15	  readonly totalCreated: number;
16	  readonly totalCompleted: number;
17	  readonly totalStopped: number;
18	  readonly totalFailed: number;
19	  readonly totalStepsExecuted: number;
20	  readonly totalStepsSucceeded: number;
21	  readonly totalStepsFailed: number;
22	  readonly totalStepsSkipped: number;
23	}
24	
25	// --- State snapshot ---
26	
27	export interface TaskStateSnapshot {
28	  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
29	  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
30	  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
31	}
32	
33	export type ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot = ReadonlyDeep<TaskStateSnapshot>;
34	
35	// --- Container ---
36	
37	export interface TaskStateContainer {
38	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
39	  createInstance(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
40	  updateInstance(
41	    instanceId: string,
42	    updates: Partial<Omit<TaskInstanceState, 'instanceId' | 'definitionRef'>>,
43	  ): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
44	  removeInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
45	  addStepResult(instanceId: string, result: TaskStepResult): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
46	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
47	  moveToHistory(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
48	  resetStats(): TaskStateSnapshot;
49	}
50	
51	// --- Helpers ---
52	
53	function emptyStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
54	  return {
55	    totalCreated: 0,
56	    totalCompleted: 0,
57	    totalStopped: 0,
58	    totalFailed: 0,
59	    totalStepsExecuted: 0,
60	    totalStepsSucceeded: 0,
61	    totalStepsFailed: 0,
62	    totalStepsSkipped: 0,
63	  };
64	}
65	
66	function isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean {
67	  switch (result.kind) {
68	    case 'send':
69	      return result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent';
70	    case 'wait-condition':
71	      return !result.matched && result.timedOut;
72	    case 'delay':
73	      return !result.completed;
74	  }
75	}
76	
77	// --- Factory ---
78	
79	export function createTaskState(
80	  initialValue?: { readonly snapshot?: ReadonlyTaskStateSnapshot },
81	): TaskStateContainer {
82	  const instances = new Map<string, TaskInstanceState>();
83	  let history: TaskInstanceState[] = [];
84	  let statistics = emptyStatistics();
85	
86	  if (initialValue?.snapshot) {
87	    for (const inst of initialValue.snapshot.instances) {
88	      instances.set(inst.instanceId, cloneInstanceState(inst));
89	    }
90	    history = initialValue.snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState);
91	    statistics = { ...initialValue.snapshot.statistics };
92	  }
93	
94	  function getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot {
95	    return {
96	      instances: [...instances.values()].map(cloneInstanceState),
97	      history: history.map(cloneInstanceState),
98	      statistics: { ...statistics },
99	    };
100	  }
101	
102	  return {
103	    getSnapshot,
104	
105	    createInstance(instanceId, definition) {
106	      const instance: TaskInstanceState = {
107	        instanceId,
108	        definitionRef: definition,
109	        lifecycle: 'created',
110	        currentStepIndex: 0,
111	        currentIteration: 0,
112	        stepResults: [],
113	      };
114	      instances.set(instanceId, instance);
115	      statistics = { ...statistics, totalCreated: statistics.totalCreated + 1 };
116	      return cloneInstanceState(instance);
117	    },
118	
119	    updateInstance(instanceId, updates) {
120	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
121	      if (!existing) return undefined;
122	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
123	        ...existing,
124	        ...updates,
125	        instanceId: existing.instanceId,
126	        definitionRef: existing.definitionRef,
127	      };
128	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
129	
130	      if (updates.lifecycle === 'completed') {
131	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalCompleted: statistics.totalCompleted + 1 };
132	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'stopped') {
133	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalStopped: statistics.totalStopped + 1 };
134	      } else if (updates.lifecycle === 'failed') {
135	        statistics = { ...statistics, totalFailed: statistics.totalFailed + 1 };
136	      }
137	
138	      return cloneInstanceState(updated);
139	    },
140	
141	    removeInstance(instanceId) {
142	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
143	      if (!existing) return undefined;
144	      instances.delete(instanceId);
145	      return cloneInstanceState(existing);
146	    },
147	
148	    addStepResult(instanceId, result) {
149	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
150	      if (!existing) return undefined;
151	
152	      const newResults = [...existing.stepResults, cloneStepResult(result)];
153	      const maxIter = Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration);
154	      const minIteration = Math.max(0, maxIter - DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS + 1);
155	      const boundedResults = newResults.filter((r) => r.iteration >= minIteration);
156	
157	      const updated: TaskInstanceState = {
158	        ...existing,
159	        stepResults: boundedResults,
160	        currentStepIndex: result.stepIndex,
161	        currentIteration: Math.max(existing.currentIteration, result.iteration),
162	      };
163	      instances.set(instanceId, updated);
164	
165	      const isSkipped = result.appliedPolicy === 'skip-step';
166	      const isFailed = !isSkipped && isStepResultFailed(result);
167	      statistics = {
168	        ...statistics,
169	        totalStepsExecuted: statistics.totalStepsExecuted + 1,
170	        totalStepsSucceeded: statistics.totalStepsSucceeded + (isSkipped || isFailed ? 0 : 1),
171	        totalStepsFailed: statistics.totalStepsFailed + (isFailed ? 1 : 0),
172	        totalStepsSkipped: statistics.totalStepsSkipped + (isSkipped ? 1 : 0),
173	      };
174	
175	      return cloneInstanceState(updated);
176	    },
177	
178	    getInstance(instanceId) {
179	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
180	      return existing ? cloneInstanceState(existing) : undefined;
181	    },
182	
183	    moveToHistory(instanceId) {
184	      const existing = instances.get(instanceId);
185	      if (!existing) return undefined;
186	      instances.delete(instanceId);
187	      history = [...history, cloneInstanceState(existing)].slice(-MAX_HISTORY);
188	      return cloneInstanceState(existing);
189	    },
190	
191	    resetStats() {
192	      statistics = emptyStatistics();
193	      return getSnapshot();
194	    },
195	  };
196	}
197	
```

1	export const SEND_SOURCES = ['user', 'task', 'scoe', 'test'] as const;
2	export type SendSource = (typeof SEND_SOURCES)[number];
3	
4	export const CHECKSUM_KINDS = ['none', 'sum8', 'xor8', 'crc16-modbus'] as const;
5	export type ChecksumKind = (typeof CHECKSUM_KINDS)[number];
6	
7	export const SEND_RESULT_KINDS = [
8	  'sent',
9	  'build-error',
10	  'target-unavailable',
11	  'transport-error',
12	  'timeout',
13	  'cancelled',
14	] as const;
15	export type SendResultKind = (typeof SEND_RESULT_KINDS)[number];
16	
17	export type SendFieldValue = string | number | boolean;
18	
19	export type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
20	
21	// --- Context ---

29	// --- Request ---

34	  readonly targetId: string;
35	  readonly options: SendOptions;

42	  readonly context: SendContext;
43	}
44	
45	export interface SendOptions {

47	  readonly autoChecksum?: boolean;

49	
50	// --- Encoding input (service translates frame snapshot to this) ---
51

53	  readonly id: string;

90	// --- Result ---
91	
92	export interface SendError {

96	
97	export interface SendResult {

103	  readonly error?: SendError;

105	}
106	
107	// --- Statistics ---
108	
109	export interface SendCategoryStats {

114	export interface SendStatisticsSnapshot {

120	  readonly byTarget: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<SendCategoryStats>>>;
121	  readonly byResultKind: Readonly<Partial<Record<SendResultKind, number>>>;
122	  readonly lastSendAt?: string;
123	}
124	
125	export interface SendStatsDelta {

129	  readonly bytesSent: number;
130	  readonly timestamp: string;
131	}
132	
133	// --- State ---
134	
135	export type SendServiceStatus = 'idle' | 'sending' | 'error';
136	
137	export interface SendStateSnapshot {
138	  readonly status: SendServiceStatus;
139	  readonly statistics: SendStatisticsSnapshot;
140	  readonly recentResults: readonly SendResult[];
141	  readonly lastError?: SendError;
142	}

1	import type {
2	  TaskInstanceState,
3	  TaskProgress,
4	} from '../core';
5	import { calculateProgress, cloneInstanceState } from '../core';
6	import type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';
7	
8	export function selectTaskInstances(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {
9	  return snapshot.instances.map(cloneInstanceState);
10	}
11	
12	export function selectTaskInstance(
13	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
14	  instanceId: string,
15	): TaskInstanceState | undefined {
16	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
17	  return instance ? cloneInstanceState(instance) : undefined;
18	}
19	
20	export function selectTaskProgress(
21	  snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot,
22	  instanceId: string,
23	): TaskProgress | undefined {
24	  const instance = snapshot.instances.find((i) => i.instanceId === instanceId);
25	  return instance ? calculateProgress(instance) : undefined;
26	}
27	
28	export function selectTaskHistory(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskInstanceState[] {
29	  return snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState);
30	}
31	
32	export function selectTaskStatistics(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskStatisticsSnapshot {
33	  return { ...snapshot.statistics };
34	}
35	
36	export interface TaskUiSnapshot {
37	  readonly instances: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
38	  readonly history: readonly TaskInstanceState[];
39	  readonly statistics: TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
40	}
41	
42	export function selectTaskSnapshot(snapshot: TaskStateSnapshot): TaskUiSnapshot {
43	  return {
44	    instances: snapshot.instances.map(cloneInstanceState),
45	    history: snapshot.history.map(cloneInstanceState),
46	    statistics: { ...snapshot.statistics },
47	  };
48	}
49

11	// --- Helper factories ---

13	function sendStep(overrides: { id: string; frameId?: string; targetId?: string }): TaskStepDefinition {
14	  return {
15	    id: overrides.id,
16	    kind: 'send',
17	    sendConfig: {

21	    },
22	  };
23	}
24	
25	function waitConditionStep(id: string, condition: WaitCondition, timeoutMs = 5000): TaskStepDefinition {
26	  return {
27	    id,
28	    kind: 'wait-condition',
29	    waitConfig: { condition, timeoutMs, onTimeout: 'fail' },
30	  };
31	}
32	
33	function delayStep(id: string, durationMs = 1000): TaskStepDefinition {
34	  return {
35	    id,
36	    kind: 'delay',
37	    delayConfig: { durationMs },
38	  };
39	}
40

1	import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
2	import {
3	  canTransition,
4	  transition,
5	  isTerminal,
6	  evaluateCondition,
7	  compareValues,

14	import type { TaskLifecycleStatus, TaskProgress, TaskExecutionSummary, TaskStepResult } from '../core';

28	
29	// ============================================================
30	// Lifecycle
31	// ============================================================
32	
33	describe('Task lifecycle', () => {
34	  describe('canTransition', () => {
35	    it('allows created -> running', () => {
36	      expect(canTransition('created', 'running')).toBe(true);
37	    });
38	
39	    it('allows running -> paused', () => {
40	      expect(canTransition('running', 'paused')).toBe(true);
41	    });
42	
43	    it('allows running -> stopped', () => {
44	      expect(canTransition('running', 'stopped')).toBe(true);
45	    });
46	
47	    it('allows running -> completed', () => {
48	      expect(canTransition('running', 'completed')).toBe(true);
49	    });
50	
51	    it('allows running -> failed', () => {
52	      expect(canTransition('running', 'failed')).toBe(true);
53	    });
54	
55	    it('allows paused -> running', () => {
56	      expect(canTransition('paused', 'running')).toBe(true);
57	    });
58	
59	    it('allows paused -> stopped', () => {
60	      expect(canTransition('paused', 'stopped')).toBe(true);
61	    });
62	
63	    it('rejects created -> paused', () => {
64	      expect(canTransition('created', 'paused')).toBe(false);
65	    });
66	
67	    it('rejects created -> completed', () => {
68	      expect(canTransition('created', 'completed')).toBe(false);
69	    });
70	
71	    it('rejects created -> stopped', () => {
72	      expect(canTransition('created', 'stopped')).toBe(false);
73	    });
74	
75	    it('rejects stopped -> running', () => {
76	      expect(canTransition('stopped', 'running')).toBe(false);
77	    });
78	
79	    it('rejects completed -> running', () => {
80	      expect(canTransition('completed', 'running')).toBe(false);
81	    });
82	
83	    it('rejects failed -> running', () => {
84	      expect(canTransition('failed', 'running')).toBe(false);
85	    });
86	
87	    it('rejects running -> created', () => {
88	      expect(canTransition('running', 'created')).toBe(false);
89	    });
90	  });
91	
92	  describe('transition', () => {
93	    it('transitions created -> running on start', () => {
94	      expect(transition('created', 'start')).toBe('running');
95	    });
96	
97	    it('transitions running -> paused on pause', () => {
98	      expect(transition('running', 'pause')).toBe('paused');
99	    });
100	
101	    it('transitions paused -> running on resume', () => {
102	      expect(transition('paused', 'resume')).toBe('running');
103	    });
104	
105	    it('transitions running -> stopped on stop', () => {
106	      expect(transition('running', 'stop')).toBe('stopped');
107	    });
108	
109	    it('transitions running -> completed on complete', () => {
110	      expect(transition('running', 'complete')).toBe('completed');
111	    });
112	
113	    it('transitions running -> failed on fail', () => {
114	      expect(transition('running', 'fail')).toBe('failed');
115	    });
116	
117	    it('transitions paused -> stopped on stop', () => {
118	      expect(transition('paused', 'stop')).toBe('stopped');
119	    });
120	
121	    it('throws on created -> pause (illegal)', () => {
122	      expect(() => transition('created', 'pause')).toThrow(/Invalid lifecycle transition/);
123	    });
124	
125	    it('throws on stopped -> resume (illegal)', () => {
126	      expect(() => transition('stopped', 'resume')).toThrow(/Invalid lifecycle transition/);
127	    });
128	
129	    it('throws on completed -> start (illegal)', () => {
130	      expect(() => transition('completed', 'start')).toThrow(/Invalid lifecycle transition/);
131	    });
132	
133	    it('throws on failed -> resume (illegal)', () => {
134	      expect(() => transition('failed', 'resume')).toThrow(/Invalid lifecycle transition/);
135	    });
136	  });
137	
138	  describe('isTerminal', () => {
139	    it.each(['stopped', 'completed', 'failed'] satisfies TaskLifecycleStatus[])(
140	      'returns true for %s',
141	      (status) => {
142	        expect(isTerminal(status)).toBe(true);
143	      },
144	    );
145	
146	    it.each(['created', 'running', 'paused'] satisfies TaskLifecycleStatus[])(
147	      'returns false for %s',
148	      (status) => {
149	        expect(isTerminal(status)).toBe(false);
150	      },
151	    );
152	  });
153	});
154	
155	// ============================================================
156	// ConditionMatcher
157	// ============================================================
158	
159	describe('ConditionMatcher', () => {
160	  describe('eq operator', () => {
161	    it('matches equal numeric value', () => {
162	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.eqNumeric(), matchInputs.matchingNumeric())).toBe(true);
163	    });
164	
165	    it('does not match different numeric value', () => {
166	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.eqNumeric(), matchInputs.nonMatchingNumeric())).toBe(false);
167	    });
168	
169	    it('matches equal string value', () => {
170	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.eqString(), matchInputs.matchingString())).toBe(true);
171	    });
172	
173	    it('does not match null value', () => {
174	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.eqNumeric(), matchInputs.nullValue())).toBe(false);
175	    });
176	  });
177	
178	  describe('neq operator', () => {
179	    it('matches different value', () => {
180	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.neq(), matchInputs.matchingNumeric())).toBe(true);
181	    });
182	
183	    it('does not match equal value', () => {
184	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.neq(), matchInputs.nonMatchingNumeric())).toBe(false);
185	    });
186	  });
187	
188	  describe('gt operator', () => {
189	    it('matches when value > threshold', () => {
190	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.gt(), matchInputs.matchingNumeric())).toBe(true);
191	    });
192	
193	    it('does not match when value <= threshold', () => {
194	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.gt(), matchInputs.nonMatchingNumeric())).toBe(false);
195	    });
196	  });
197	
198	  describe('lt operator', () => {
199	    it('matches when value < threshold', () => {
200	      const input = { frameId: 'frame-1', fieldId: 'field-1', value: 25 };
201	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.lt(), input)).toBe(true);
202	    });
203	
204	    it('does not match when value == threshold', () => {
205	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.lt(), matchInputs.nonMatchingNumeric())).toBe(false);
206	    });
207	
208	    it('does not match when value > threshold', () => {
209	      expect(evaluateCondition(waitConditions.lt(), matchInputs.matchingNumeric())).toBe(false);

1	import {
2	  buildFrame,
3	  validateSendRequest,
4	} from '../core';
5	import type {
6	  ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
7	  SendBuildIssue,
8	  SendError,
9	  SendFieldEncodingDef,
10	  SendRequest,
11	  SendRequestRef,
12	  SendResult,
13	  SendResultKind,
14	  SendServiceStatus,
15	  SendStateSnapshot,
16	  SendStatisticsSnapshot,
17	} from '../core';
18	import type { ReadonlyFrameAsset } from '@/features/frame';
19	import type {
20	  SendFrameReader,
21	  SendResultEmitter,
22	  SendTargetResolver,
23	  SendTransportWriteOutcome,
24	  SendTransportWriter,
25	} from '../adapters';
26	import { createSendState } from '../state';
27	import type { SendStateContainer } from '../state';
28	import {
29	  selectSendResults,
30	  selectSendSnapshot,
31	  selectSendStatistics,
32	  selectSendStatus,
33	} from '../selectors';
34	
35	export interface SendReader {
36	  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
37	  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;
38	  listResults(): SendResult[];
39	  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;
40	}
41	
42	export interface SendService extends SendReader {
43	  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
44	  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
45	}
46	
47	export interface CreateSendServiceOptions {
48	  readonly frameReader: SendFrameReader;
49	  readonly targetResolver: SendTargetResolver;
50	  readonly transportWriter: SendTransportWriter;
51	  readonly resultEmitter?: SendResultEmitter;
52	  readonly state?: SendStateContainer;
53	  readonly now?: () => string;
54	}
55	
56	function defaultNow(): string {
57	  return new Date().toISOString();
58	}
59	
60	function toRequestRef(request: SendRequest): SendRequestRef {
61	  return {
62	    frameId: request.frameId,
63	    targetId: request.targetId,
64	    context: { ...request.context },
65	  };
66	}
67	
68	function makeResult(
69	  kind: SendResultKind,
70	  requestRef: SendRequestRef,
71	  timestamp: string,
72	  bytesBuilt: number,
73	  bytesSent: number,
74	  error?: SendError,
75	  buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[] = [],
76	): SendResult {
77	  return {
78	    kind,
79	    requestRef,
80	    bytesBuilt,
81	    bytesSent,
82	    timestamp,
83	    ...(error ? { error } : {}),
84	    buildIssues,
85	  };
86	}
87	
88	function frameToBuildInput(
89	  frame: ReadonlyFrameAsset,
90	): { fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[]; totalByteLength: number } {
91	  const fields: SendFieldEncodingDef[] = [];
92	  let offset = 0;
93	  const frameEndian = frame.options?.bigEndian ?? false;
94	
95	  for (const field of frame.fields) {
96	    fields.push({
97	      id: field.id,
98	      dataType: field.dataType,
99	      length: field.length,
100	      bigEndian: field.bigEndian ?? frameEndian,
101	      isASCII: field.isASCII ?? false,
102	      offset,
103	    });
104	    offset += field.length;
105	  }
106	
107	  return { fields, totalByteLength: offset };
108	}
109	
110	export function createSendReader(
111	  snapshotProvider: () => ReadonlySendStateSnapshot,
112	): SendReader {
113	  return {
114	    getSnapshot() {
115	      return selectSendSnapshot(snapshotProvider());
116	    },
117	    getStatistics() {
118	      return selectSendStatistics(snapshotProvider());
119	    },
120	    listResults() {
121	      return selectSendResults(snapshotProvider());
122	    },
123	    getStatus() {
124	      return selectSendStatus(snapshotProvider());
125	    },
126	  };
127	}
128	
129	export function createSendService(options: CreateSendServiceOptions): SendService {
130	  const state = options.state ?? createSendState();
131	  const now = options.now ?? defaultNow;
132	  const reader = createSendReader(() => state.getSnapshot());
133	
134	  return {
135	    ...reader,
136	
137	    async execute(request) {
138	      const requestRef = toRequestRef(request);
139	      const timestamp = now();
140	
141	      state.setStatus('sending');
142	
143	      // 1. Validate request
144	      const validationIssues = validateSendRequest(request);
145	      const hasErrors = validationIssues.some((i) => i.severity === 'error');
146	      if (hasErrors) {
147	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, validationIssues);
148	        state.addResult(result);
149	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
150	        return result;
151	      }
152	
153	      // 2. Resolve frame snapshot
154	      const frame = options.frameReader.getFrame(request.frameId);
155	      if (!frame) {
156	        const result = makeResult(
157	          'build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0,
158	          { kind: 'frame-not-found', message: `Frame not found: ${request.frameId}` },
159	        );
160	        state.addResult(result);
161	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
162	        return result;
163	      }
164	
165	      // 3. Build frame bytes
166	      const { fields, totalByteLength } = frameToBuildInput(frame);
167	      const buildOutput = buildFrame({
168	        fields,
169	        totalByteLength,
170	        fieldValues: request.fieldValues,
171	      });
172	
173	      const buildErrors = buildOutput.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error');
174	      if (buildErrors.length > 0) {
175	        const result = makeResult('build-error', requestRef, timestamp, 0, 0, undefined, buildOutput.issues);
176	        state.addResult(result);
177	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
178	        return result;
179	      }
180	
181	      const bytes = [...buildOutput.bytes];
182	
183	      // 4. Resolve target
184	      const target = options.targetResolver.resolveTarget(request.targetId);
185	      if (!target) {
186	        const result = makeResult(
187	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
188	          { kind: 'target-not-found', message: `Target not found: ${request.targetId}` },
189	        );
190	        state.addResult(result);
191	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
192	        return result;
193	      }
194	
195	      if (!target.available) {
196	        const result = makeResult(
197	          'target-unavailable', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
198	          { kind: 'target-not-available', message: `Target not available: ${request.targetId}` },
199	        );
200	        state.addResult(result);
201	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
202	        return result;
203	      }
204	
205	      // 5. Transport write
206	      let writeOutcome: SendTransportWriteOutcome;
207	      try {
208	        writeOutcome = await options.transportWriter.writeBytes(target.connectionId, bytes);
209	      } catch (err) {
210	        const result = makeResult(
211	          'transport-error', requestRef, timestamp, bytes.length, 0,
212	          { kind: 'write-exception', message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unknown write error' },
213	          buildOutput.issues,
214	        );
215	        state.addResult(result);
216	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
217	        return result;
218	      }
219	
220	      if (!writeOutcome.ok) {
221	        const result = makeResult(
222	          writeOutcome.error?.kind === 'timeout' ? 'timeout' : 'transport-error',
223	          requestRef,
224	          timestamp,
225	          bytes.length,
226	          writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
227	          writeOutcome.error,
228	          buildOutput.issues,
229	        );
230	        state.addResult(result);
231	        options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
232	        return result;
233	      }
234	
235	      // 6. Success
236	      const result = makeResult(
237	        'sent',
238	        requestRef,
239	        timestamp,
240	        bytes.length,
241	        writeOutcome.bytesWritten,
242	        undefined,
243	        buildOutput.issues,
244	      );
245	      state.addResult(result);
246	      options.resultEmitter?.emit(result);
247	      return result;
248	    },
249	
250	    resetStats() {
251	      return state.resetStats();
252	    },
253	  };
254	}
255	
```

1	import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
2	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { TaskDefinition } from '../core';
4	import { timedTaskDef, triggerTaskDef, sequenceTaskDef, scoeModeTaskDef, errorPolicies, makeSendResult } from '../fixtures/task-fixtures';
5	import { createFakeSendService } from '../adapters';
6	import { createFakeReceiveEventSource } from '../adapters/fake-receive-event-source';
7	import { createTaskService, type TaskService } from '../services/task-service';
8	import { createTaskState, type TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
9	import { selectTaskSnapshot, selectTaskStatistics, selectTaskProgress, selectTaskInstance, selectTaskHistory } from '../selectors/task-selectors';
10	
11	// --- Helpers ---
12	
13	function makeSentResult(): SendResult {
14	  return makeSendResult();
15	}
16	
17	function makeErrorResult(kind: 'transport-error' | 'timeout' | 'target-unavailable' | 'build-error' = 'transport-error'): SendResult {
18	  return makeSendResult({ kind }) as SendResult;
19	}
20	
21	function createTestSetup(overrides: { definition?: TaskDefinition; sendResults?: readonly SendResult[] } = {}) {
22	  const fakeSend = createFakeSendService({ results: overrides.sendResults ?? [makeSentResult()] });
23	  const fakeReceive = createFakeReceiveEventSource();
24	  const service = createTaskService({
25	    sendService: fakeSend,
26	    receiveEventSource: fakeReceive,
27	    now: () => '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z',
28	  });
29	  const definition = overrides.definition ?? timedTaskDef();
30	  const instance = service.createTask(definition);
31	  return { service, fakeSend, fakeReceive, definition, instance };
32	}
33	
34	async function settle(service: TaskService, instanceId: string, timeoutMs = 2000): Promise<void> {
35	  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
36	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
37	    const inst = service.getInstance(instanceId);
38	    if (!inst || inst.lifecycle === 'completed' || inst.lifecycle === 'stopped' || inst.lifecycle === 'failed' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
39	      return;
40	    }
41	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
42	  }
43	}

49	describe('TaskService - timed scheduling', () => {
50	  it('executes N iterations then completes', async () => {
51	    const maxCount = 3;
52	    const def: TaskDefinition = { ...timedTaskDef(), maxCount, intervalMs: 10 };
53	    const results = Array.from({ length: maxCount * def.steps.length }, () => makeSentResult());
54	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
55	
56	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
57	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId);
58	
59	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
60	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
61	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(maxCount * def.steps.length);
62	  });
63	
64	  it('tracks iterations in step results', async () => {
65	    const maxCount = 2;
66	    const def: TaskDefinition = { ...timedTaskDef(), maxCount, intervalMs: 10 };
67	    const results = Array.from({ length: maxCount * def.steps.length }, () => makeSentResult());
68	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
69	
70	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
71	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId);
72	
73	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
74	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
75	    const iterations = new Set(final!.stepResults.map((r) => r.iteration));
76	    expect(iterations.size).toBe(maxCount);
77	  });
78	});

143	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
144	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 10));

146	    // Emit non-matching event

148	      frameId: 'frame-1',

153	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
154	
155	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(0);

157	    service.stopTask(instance.instanceId);

176	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);

237	describe('TaskService - SCOE pattern', () => {
238	  it('send → wait-condition → send → completed', async () => {
239	    const def = scoeModeTaskDef();
240	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()]; // 2 send steps
241	    const { service, fakeReceive, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({
242	      definition: def,
243	      sendResults: results,
244	    });
245	
246	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
247	    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
248	
249	    // First send step should have executed
250	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(1);
251	
252	    // Emit matching condition for wait-condition-step
253	    const waitStep = def.steps[1];
254	    if (waitStep.kind === 'wait-condition') {
255	      fakeReceive.emit({
256	        frameId: waitStep.waitConfig.condition.frameId,
257	        fieldId: waitStep.waitConfig.condition.fieldId,
258	        value: waitStep.waitConfig.condition.threshold,
259	      });
260	    }
261	
262	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
263	
264	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
265	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
266	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2); // command + ack
267	    expect(final!.stepResults.length).toBe(3);
268	    expect(final!.stepResults[0].kind).toBe('send');
269	    expect(final!.stepResults[1].kind).toBe('wait-condition');
270	    expect(final!.stepResults[1].matched).toBe(true);
271	    expect(final!.stepResults[2].kind).toBe('send');
272	  });
273	});

288	        },

291	    };

303	    if (waitResult?.kind === 'wait-condition') {
304	      expect(waitResult.matched).toBe(false);
305	      expect(waitResult.timedOut).toBe(true);

309	  it('onTimeout=fail triggers error policy', async () => {

317	        },
318	      ],
319	    };

322	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);

328	
329	  it('onTimeout=skip skips the step', async () => {
330	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
331	      ...scoeModeTaskDef(),
332	      steps: [
333	        { id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } },
334	        {
335	          id: 's2', kind: 'wait-condition',
336	          waitConfig: { condition: { frameId: 'f1', fieldId: 'f1', operator: 'eq', threshold: 99 }, timeoutMs: 50, onTimeout: 'skip' },
337	        },
338	        { id: 's3', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
339	      ],
340	    };
341	    const results = [makeSentResult(), makeSentResult()];
342	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
343	
344	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
345	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 500);
346	
347	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
348	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
349	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(2);
350	    const waitResult = final!.stepResults.find((r) => r.kind === 'wait-condition');
351	    expect(waitResult?.appliedPolicy).toBe('skip-step');
352	  });
353	});

412	      steps: [{ id: 's1', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f1', fieldValues: { f: 1 } } }],
413	      errorPolicy: errorPolicies.retryTwice(),
414	    };
415	    const results = Array.from({ length: 10 }, () => makeErrorResult());
416	    const { service, fakeSend, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
417	
418	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);
419	    await settle(service, instance.instanceId, 2000);
420	
421	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
422	    expect(final?.lifecycle).toBe('failed');
423	    // 1 original + 2 retries = 3 calls
424	    expect(fakeSend.calls.length).toBe(3);
425	  });
426	
427	  it('retry policy succeeds on retry', async () => {
428	    const def: TaskDefinition = {
429	      id: 'retry-succeed',
430	      name: 'Retry Succeed',
431	      schedulingMode: 'sequence',
432	      triggerSource: 'user-ui',

435	        { id: 's2', kind: 'send', sendConfig: { frameId: 'f2', fieldValues: { f: 2 } } },
436	      ],
437	      errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'retry', retryCount: 2, retryDelayMs: 10 },
438	    };
439	    // First call fails, second succeeds, third succeeds

441	    const { service, instance } = createTestSetup({ definition: def, sendResults: results });
442	
443	    service.startTask(instance.instanceId);

446	    const final = service.getInstance(instance.instanceId);

530	describe('TaskService - stop', () => {
531	  it('stop results in lifecycle=stopped (not completed)', async () => {

548	// ========================================================================
549	// State / Selectors
550	// ========================================================================
551	
552	describe('Task state and selectors', () => {
553	  let state: TaskStateContainer;
554	
555	  beforeEach(() => {
556	    state = createTaskState();
557	  });
558	
559	  it('createInstance adds to instances', () => {
560	    const def = timedTaskDef();
561	    const inst = state.createInstance('inst-1', def);
562	    expect(inst.instanceId).toBe('inst-1');
563	    expect(inst.lifecycle).toBe('created');
564	
565	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
566	    expect(snapshot.instances.length).toBe(1);
567	    expect(snapshot.instances[0].instanceId).toBe('inst-1');
568	  });
569	
570	  it('updateInstance modifies lifecycle', () => {
571	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
572	    const updated = state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'running', startedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
573	    expect(updated?.lifecycle).toBe('running');
574	    expect(updated?.startedAt).toBe('2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z');
575	  });
576	
577	  it('addStepResult appends and bounds history', () => {
578	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
579	    for (let i = 0; i < 150; i++) {
580	      state.addStepResult('inst-1', {
581	        kind: 'send',
582	        stepIndex: 0,
583	        iteration: i,
584	        sendResult: makeSentResult(),
585	      });
586	    }
587	    const inst = state.getInstance('inst-1')!;
588	    // Should keep only results from iterations >= 50 (150 - 100 + 1)
589	    const minIter = Math.max(0, 149 - 100 + 1);
590	    expect(inst.stepResults.length).toBe(149 - minIter + 1);
591	    expect(inst.stepResults.every((r) => r.iteration >= minIter)).toBe(true);
592	  });
593	
594	  it('moveToHistory moves terminal instances', () => {
595	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
596	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'completed', completedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
597	    state.moveToHistory('inst-1');
598	
599	    const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
600	    expect(snapshot.instances.length).toBe(0);
601	    expect(snapshot.history.length).toBe(1);
602	    expect(snapshot.history[0].instanceId).toBe('inst-1');
603	  });
604	
605	  it('removeInstance removes from state', () => {
606	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
607	    state.removeInstance('inst-1');
608	    expect(state.getInstance('inst-1')).toBeUndefined();
609	  });
610	
611	  it('getSnapshot returns independent clones', () => {
612	    state.createInstance('inst-1', timedTaskDef());
613	    const s1 = state.getSnapshot();
614	    const s2 = state.getSnapshot();
615	    expect(s1).toEqual(s2);
616	    expect(s1.instances).not.toBe(s2.instances);
617	  });
618	
619	  it('statistics update correctly', () => {
620	    const def = timedTaskDef();
621	    state.createInstance('inst-1', def);
622	    expect(state.getSnapshot().statistics.totalCreated).toBe(1);
623	
624	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'running', startedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:00.000Z' });
625	    state.updateInstance('inst-1', { lifecycle: 'completed', completedAt: '2026-05-06T12:00:01.000Z' });

# Task Feature 实现审查报告

## Contract

- **Direct contract:** `rewrite-task-design.md`, `rewrite-task-checklist.yaml`, `2026-05-06-ta[REDACTED_SK].md`, `2026-05-06-outbound-routing-and-response-decisions.md`, `rewrite-execution-charter.md`, `rewrite-quality-rules.md`, `rewrite-review-checklist.md`
- **Boundary guards:** `rewrite-target-structure.md`, send/receive/frame/connection public API 代码, handoff

## Part 1: Task 实现质量

### 1. core/types.ts 与 design 对齐

**与 design §4.3 一致的部分：**
- TaskStepKind (send/wait-condition/delay) ✅
- WaitCondition (frameId + fieldId + operator + threshold + sourceId) ✅
- TaskTriggerSource (5 种) ✅
- TaskSchedulingMode (timed/trigger/sequence) ✅
- TaskDefinition 完整字段 ✅
- TaskLifecycleStatus (6 种终态正确：stopped ≠ completed) ✅
- TaskInstanceState, TaskStepResult 多态 ✅
- ConditionMatchInput 与 receive 解耦 ✅

**与 design 的偏差：**
- TaskProgress 缺少 `estimatedRemainingMs` 和 `lastStepResult`（design §4.3 声明了这两个字段）
- TaskInstanceCompletion（design §4.3 定义为内部事实，应被 emit 给 result/status/northbound）未定义为独立类型，service 也不 emit 完成事件。当前无下游消费者，但 design 契约不完整。
- `stopCondition` (maxDurationMs, maxIterations) 在 types.ts 中定义但执行循环完全未使用

### 2. core/lifecycle.ts 状态机

所有合法转换覆盖正确：
- created→running, running→{paused,stopped,completed,failed}, paused→{running,stopped} ✅
- stopped/completed/failed 为终态，不可恢复 ✅
- stop 结果是 `stopped` 不是 `completed` ✅（charter 硬要求）
- 非法转换抛异常 ✅
- paused→stopped 正确实现 ✅（允许在暂停状态下停止）

### 3. core/condition-matcher.ts

8 种 operator 全部覆盖且逻辑正确 ✅
- frameId/fieldId 过滤 ✅
- sourceId 过滤 ✅（但有边界 gap，见 Medium #3）
- compareValues 处理 number/number, string/string, null, mixed types ✅

### 4. core/progress.ts

进度计算逻辑合理：
- 按 currentIteration 过滤 stepResults 计算当前迭代进度 ✅
- countCompletedIterations 正确计算已完成迭代数 ✅
- resolveIterationsTotal 正确区分 timed(maxCount)/sequence(1)/trigger(null) ✅

### 5. core/clone.ts

所有 clone 函数做深拷贝，切断引用共享 ✅。cloneSendResultRef 正确处理了 SendResult 的嵌套结构。

### 6. adapters/ports.ts

- `SendServiceProvider.execute` 签名与 send public `SendService.execute` 完全对齐 ✅
- `ReceiveEventSource.subscribe` 使用 task 自己的 `ConditionMatchInput`，与 receive public API 解耦 ✅（design 要求 service 层做映射）
- `FrameReader.getFrameAsset` 使用 frame 的 `ReadonlyFrameAsset` ✅
- task 不 import send/receive/frame 的 internal subpath ✅

### 7. adapters/fake-*.ts

- FakeSendService：预设结果、记录调用、延迟返回、失败便捷工厂 ✅
- FakeReceiveEventSource：手动 emit、记录、subscribe/unsubscribe ✅
- 覆盖关键场景：成功、失败、超时（通过 SendResult.kind='timeout'）、延迟 ✅

### 8. services/task-service.ts（重点）

**执行循环与 design §4.4 对齐：**
- timed：按 interval 迭代，支持 maxCount 和无限 ✅
- trigger：注册条件，按 cooldown/maxTriggerCount 控制触发 ✅
- sequence：单次执行 step 序列 ✅

**多态 step 执行：**
- send-step：构建 SendRequest → await sendService → 记录结果 ✅
- wait-condition-step：注册到 ConditionRegistry → Promise.race(匹配/超时/中断) ✅
- delay-step：Promise.race(sleep/中断) ✅

**Error policy 四种策略：**
- retry：遵守 retryCount + retryDelayMs，耗尽后 fail ✅
- skip-step：记录 appliedPolicy，继续 ✅
- stop：设 lifecycle=stopped ✅
- pause：设 lifecycle=pause ✅

**onTimeout + errorPolicy 两层交互：**
- continue → step 通过，errorPolicy 不介入 ✅
- skip → appliedPolicy='skip-step'，errorPolicy 不介入 ✅
- fail → 交给 errorPolicy 决定 ✅

**trigger 外层与 wait-condition 内层互不干扰：** triggerCount 在 executeSteps 前递增，step 失败不影响 trigger 计数 ✅

**Pause/resume：** pause 清除条件注册和 abort signal，resume 从保存的 iteration 继续 ✅。但 resume 跳过已处理的 step（包括因失败导致 pause 的 step），不会重试该 step（design 未明确此处语义）。

**Stop：** lifecycle 设为 `stopped` ✅，不设 completed ✅

**ConditionRegistry：** Map<frameId, Set<Entry>>，O(1) 查找 ✅

**Subscription 管理：** ensureSubscription/releaseSubscription 引用计数 ✅

### 9. state/task-state.ts + selectors/task-selectors.ts

- State 单点写入（只有 task service 写入）✅
- Selector 返回 clone/spread 副本，不暴露可变引用 ✅
- stepResults 有界（DEFAULT_MAX_STEP_RESULT_ITERATIONS=100）✅
- history 有界（MAX_HISTORY=50）✅
- 统计独立于 send/receive ✅

### 10. index.ts 公共 API

- 导出：core types + service factory/interface + state types (read-only) + selectors ✅
- 不导出：mutable state container、fake adapter 默认实例、internal state creators ✅

### 11. fixtures/task-fixtures.ts

- timed/trigger/sequence/SCOE 四种模式 fixture ✅
- 所有 8 种 operator 的 WaitCondition fixture ✅
- 4 种 errorPolicy fixture ✅
- 各种 match input（matching/non-matching/null/differentFrame/withSource）✅
- Helper factories 覆盖 send/wait/delay step result ✅

---

## Part 2: Send/Task 交叉检查

### task → send 对齐

- task ports 的 `SendServiceProvider.execute(SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>` 与 send public `SendService.execute` 完全匹配 ✅
- task 只 import `SendRequest` 和 `SendResult` 类型（来自 `@/features/send`，即 send/index.ts）✅
- task 不 import send 内部的 state/adapter/service/core ✅
- buildSendRequest 正确构建 SendRequest（frameId, fieldValues, targetId fallback, options, context）✅
- send 不了解 task 的存在 ✅

### task → receive 对齐

- task 的 `ReceiveEventSource` port 完全与 receive 解耦，使用自己的 `ConditionMatchInput` ✅
- task 不 import receive 的任何东西 ✅（完美解耦）
- runtime 层负责将 receive 的 `ReceiveInputEvent` 映射为 task 的 `ConditionMatchInput` ✅

### task → frame 对齐

- task ports 只 import `ReadonlyFrameAsset`（来自 frame public API）✅
- 不访问 frame 内部 state ✅

### send 不知道 task

send 代码中无任何 task 引用 ✅

---

## Findings

### High

**H1: settleResolvers 泄漏 — stop 后 onSettled 永远挂起**

`task-service.ts:699-706`。`onSettled()` 创建 Promise 存入 settleResolvers，只有 `completeTask` 和 `failTask` 会 resolve 它。但 `stopTask` 不调用 resolveSettle。如果外部 await `service.onSettled(id)` 后任务被 stop，该 Promise 永远不 resolve。

修复建议：`stopTask` 中也调用 `resolveSettle`，或 `runExecutionLoop` finally 中清理。

**H2: Pause→Resume 端到端流程未测试**

`ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts` 的 "resume continues execution" 测试（L491-523）实际上是创建第二个 task 并验证 service 仍然工作，**没有测试 pause→resume 流程**。唯一测试 pause 的用例（L456-489）在 pause 后直接 stop，未验证 resume 后执行是否正确继续。

### Medium

**M1: sourceId 过滤边界 gap**

`condition-matcher.ts:10`。当 condition 指定了 sourceId 但 input 没有 sourceId 时，条件 `condition.sourceId !== undefined && input.sourceId !== undefined` 第二项为 false，整个过滤被跳过，导致匹配通过。语义上，如果 condition 要求特定 source，input 无 sourceId 时不应匹配。

**M2: delay-step 中断时 setTimeout 泄漏**

`task-service.ts:107-109`。`sleep()` 创建 setTimeout 但在 Promise.race 被中断时不会 clearTimeout。对于短 delay 无影响，但如果 delayStep 设置 60s，中断后 timer 仍挂在事件循环上直到超时。

**M3: stopCondition 定义但未实现**

`types.ts:105-108` 定义了 `TaskStopCondition`（maxDurationMs, maxIterations），`TaskDefinition` 包含可选 stopCondition 字段，但 `runTimedLoop`/`runTriggerLoop` 完全不检查它。对 timed 模式 maxCount 替代了 maxIterations，但 maxDurationMs 从未生效。

**M4: errorPolicy=pause 后 resume 跳过失败 step**

当 step 失败触发 pause 时，失败 step 的 result（含 appliedPolicy='pause'）已写入 state。Resume 后 executeSteps 从 startIndex = completedInIter.length 开始，跳过了该 step。如果意图是"暂停让用户修复后重试"，当前行为不符合。Design 未明确此处语义。

**M5: TaskProgress 缺少 design 声明的字段**

Design §4.3 声明 TaskProgress 含 `estimatedRemainingMs` 和 `lastStepResult`，实现中两者都缺失。不影响核心功能但对 UI 消费者不完整。

**M6: retry 耗尽后 lifecycle 设为 'failed'**

`task-service.ts:380`。retry 耗尽后 `updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'fail')`。Design 只说 retry 超过 retryCount 后按 fallback 处理，未明确应该 fail 还是 stop。failed 意味着不可恢复，但 retry 是用户配置的策略，用户可能期望 stop 后可以手动 resume。Design gap。

### Low

**L1: FakeReceiveEventSourceOptions 声明但未使用**

`fake-receive-event-source.ts:4-7` 定义了含 autoEmit 的 options interface，但 `createFakeReceiveEventSource()` 不接受参数。Dead interface。

**L2: makeSendResult 类型 cast 不准确**

`task-fixtures.ts:234`：`kind: (overrides.kind ?? 'sent') as 'sent'`。当 overrides.kind 存在时（如 'transport-error'），类型仍是 `'sent'`。运行时值正确但类型声明不诚实。

**L3: nextInstanceId 是模块级变量**

`task-service.ts:128`。多次调用 `createTaskService` 共享同一个计数器。不影响功能但阻止独立 service 实例有隔离的 ID 空间。

**L4: any operator + null value 导致 matchedValue 类型不安全**

`condition-matcher.ts:22-23`。`any` operator 对 null 值返回 true，但 `ConditionRegistry.processInput` 将 `input.value`（可能为 null）cast 为 `number | string` 传给 onMatch。WaitConditionStepResult.matchedValue 类型声明为 `number | string` 但实际可能为 null。

**L5: delayBeforeStartMs 未测试**

Types 和 runTimedLoop 中实现了 delayBeforeStartMs，但测试文件未覆盖。

**L6: 无 infinite timed task 测试**

Design 提到 timed 模式支持无限迭代（maxCount 为空），测试中所有 timed 用例都设了 maxCount。

---

## Review Verdict

**pass-with-known-gaps**

理由：
- 核心架构与 design 对齐：多态 step、三种调度模式、lifecycle 状态机、error policy、条件匹配、进度追踪全部实现且核心逻辑正确
- 跨 feature 边界干净：task 只通过 port 接口消费 send/receive/frame，不 import 任何 internal
- 公共 API 不泄露 mutable state、fake adapter、internal instances
- stop 结果为 stopped 不是 completed（charter 硬要求）
- 330+ tests 覆盖 lifecycle/matcher/progress/clone/service/state/selector

Known gaps 必须登记：
- H1 settleResolvers 泄漏需要修复后才能让 onSettled 安全使用
- H2 pause→resume 端到端测试缺失
- M3 stopCondition 未实现
- 无 runtime/hardware validation

不宣称：task implementation complete（需修复 H1）、real hardware task execution、SCOE task integration、northbound task control。

## 工作方式

2. 汇总后分批并行开 executor 子 agent 修代码
3. 最后开独立 verifier 子 agent 跑 test + lint + 边界检查

一次最多 3 个子 agent，一批完成后再开下一批。

## 修复清单（按优先级）

### 必须修（High）

**H1: settleResolvers 泄漏**
- 文件: `rewrite/src/features/task/services/task-service.ts`
- 问题: stopTask 不调用 resolveSettle，导致 onSettled() 永远挂起
- 修复: stopTask 中加 resolveSettle，或在 runExecutionLoop finally 中统一清理

**H2: pause→resume 端到端测试缺失**
- 文件: `rewrite/src/features/task/__tests__/ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts`
- 问题: 现有 "resume continues execution" 测试实际是创建第二个 task，没测 pause→resume 流程
- 修复: 新增测试：start → 等第一步完成 → pause → 验证 paused + progress 保留 → resume → 验证执行继续到 completed
- 建议：用 sequence 模式 + delay step（durationMs 够大以便在中间 pause）

### 建议修（Medium）

**M1: sourceId 过滤边界**
- 文件: `rewrite/src/features/task/core/condition-matcher.ts:10`
- 问题: condition 有 sourceId 但 input 没有 sourceId 时，过滤被跳过
- 修复: 当 condition.sourceId !== undefined 且 input.sourceId === undefined 时应返回 false

**M2: delay-step setTimeout 泄漏**
- 文件: `rewrite/src/features/task/services/task-service.ts:107-109`
- 问题: sleep() 的 setTimeout 在中断时不 clearTimeout
- 修复: 改为可取消的 sleep，返回 timer handle 并在 interrupt 时 clearTimeout

**M5: TaskProgress 缺少 design 字段**
- 文件: `rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts`, `core/progress.ts`
- 问题: design 声明了 estimatedRemainingMs 和 lastStepResult 但未实现
- 修复: 补充字段。estimatedRemainingMs 仅在 timed 有 maxCount 时可估算；lastStepResult 取最近一条 stepResult 的快照

### Design gap — 不修代码，只在代码中加 TODO 注释

这些项需要 design 补充决策后再修，当前只在代码中标记：

- **M3: stopCondition** — types.ts 中 TaskStopCondition 和 TaskDefinition.stopCondition 未在执行循环中使用。在 runTimedLoop/runTriggerLoop 顶部加 `// TODO: implement stopCondition (maxDurationMs, maxIterations) check per design §4.3`
- **M4: errorPolicy=pause resume 跳过失败 step** — 在 executeSteps 的 startIndex 计算处加 `// TODO: design decision needed — should resume after errorPolicy=pause re-execute the failed step?`
- **M6: retry 耗尽 → failed vs stopped** — 在 applyErrorPolicy retry 耗尽处加 `// TODO: design decision — should retry exhaustion result in 'failed' or 'stopped'?`

### 代码质量清理（子 agent 扫描）

修复完 High/Medium 后，开一个子 agent 做代码质量扫描，检查：

1. **重复的 isStepResultFailed 函数** — task-state.ts 和 task-service.ts 各有一个 isStepResultFailed，逻辑相同。考虑提取到 core/ 并复用。
2. **clone.ts 对 SendResult 的硬编码 clone** — cloneSendResultRef 手动展开 SendResult 字段，如果 send 的 SendResult 结构变化会不同步。是否有更好的方式？
3. **task-service.ts 函数体过长** — 700+ 行单文件，内部函数密集。评估是否值得拆分（如把 scheduling loops 拆到单独文件），但不要过度抽象。
4. **测试中的 settle helper** — 用 setTimeout 轮询等状态变化，是否可以用 onSettled 替代（修完 H1 后）。
5. **fake adapter 的 defaultSentResult 硬编码** — frameId/targetId 写死 'frame-1'/'target-1'，如果测试依赖不同值可能出问题。
6. **整体类型安全** — 检查是否有 `as` 类型断言可以消除。

质量清理原则：只做明确改善的，不搞形式化重构。每项改动都要有具体收益。

## 验证

修复完成后，开独立 verifier 子 agent 执行：

```bash
pnpm -C rewrite test
pnpm -C rewrite lint
```

加上边界检查：

```bash
# core 不依赖 Vue/Pinia/Electron
rg -l 'vue|pinia|electron|@/platform|fs\b|path\b|net\b|serialport' rewrite/src/features/task/core/

# task 不 import 其他 feature 内部
rg "from '@/features/(send|receive|frame|connection|scoe|status|result|report|northbound)/((core|state|services|adapters|composables|components|fixtures)/|[^/]*\.(ts)(?!$))" rewrite/src/features/task/ --include='*.ts' || echo "PASS: no cross-feature internal imports"

# index.ts 不导出 mutable state
rg "export.*createTaskState|export.*FakeSend|export.*FakeReceive" rewrite/src/features/task/index.ts || echo "PASS: no mutable/fake exports"
```

## 输出格式

修复完成后输出：

- Changed files: 每个文件改了什么
- Verify evidence: test/lint/rg 结果
- Open issues: TODO 注释列表 + 仍需 design 决策的项
```

这个提示词覆盖了 2 个 High 修 + 3 个 Medium 修 + 3 个 design gap 标记 + 6 项代码质量扫描。新对话主线程负责调度，建议分三批子 agent：第一批 explore 确认问题 → 第二批 executor 并行修代码 → 第三批 verifier 独立验证。
